HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B005","root_000638/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_04706ee3ef1bf72426b0","text":"3. **Shocks of time compressed into one event.** Lexical evidence: `ز ل ز ل B002` time’s shocks/hardships; activated by `ح د ث B005` event/calamity of time and the repeated `يَوْمَئِذٍ` in 99:4 and 99:6. Structural cue: the initial `إذا` opens a condition whose consequences are named as “on that day” twice. Assigned role: the quake is an event that gathers ordinary shocks of time into a decisive temporal crisis. Abductive move: I infer that the day-language lets the idiom of time’s shocks function without replacing the physical quake. Reading change: the ayah reads simultaneously as material shaking and as the arrival of accumulated temporal hardship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2dc94b585846384682c7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:1:2\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:1:4\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sarsıntı, daha sonra tartılıp görülecek yükleri bağlı kaldıkları yerden gevşeten bir başlangıç işlemi olarak da düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B008","root_000638/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ee99d574a942e167004","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:1:2\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:1:3\",\"root_000025\",[\"B008\"]],[\"99:1:4\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yeryüzü, yalnız hareket eden bir zemin değil, insan bedeni gibi titreyen bir özne görünümü kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000400/B002","root_000638/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4370eb540eaf0bd98387","text":"1. **Earthquake as forced testimony mechanism.** Lexical evidence: `ز ل ز ل B001` shaking disturbance; `ء ر ض B001` lower ground opposite sky; activated by `خ ر ج B002` extraction from hiddenness in 99:2, `ح د ث B003/B006` speech-report and disclosure in 99:4, and `و ح ي B001/B004` hidden/divine conveying in 99:5. Structural cue: 99:1 begins a temporal chain: shaking → output → human question → earth report → divine conveying. Assigned role: shaking is the mechanical trigger that converts silent ground into a reporting surface. Abductive move: the packet supplies sequence, not causality; I infer that disturbance loosens what had been held below so that report can occur. Reading change: the ayah is no longer only cosmic catastrophe; it is the first operation in an evidentiary procedure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5cd5316b792741d695fc","text":"99:4-5 retrospectively changed the passive quake into the first stage of an authorized testimony: the earth later reports because it was addressed. The farther surprise at 99:6 was that `ن و س/B001` and `ش ت ت/B001` carry oscillation into the human collective. That later social motion corrects a catastrophe-only reading of the fixed quake and activates `ز ل ز ل/B002` as a shock belonging to a decisive time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_687cfe0726b024f7592d","text":"99:6’s `ن و س B001` made the quake less one-directional: the earth is shaken first, but humans later appear as a swaying mass. 99:8’s `ش ر ر B004/B006` also retroactively made the shaking an act of loosening burdens and dangling attachments, not merely seismic violence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c9cb4f1bceae555f1ac","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:1:2\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:1:4\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yerin sarsılması, daha sonra insan topluluğunun yalpalayıp dağılmasında insan ölçeğinde sürer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73e3801b47a866c9ede0","text":"3. **A physical oscillation propagates into a temporal and social shock.** Fixed `ز ل ز ل/B002` supplies \"time's shocks,\" and the later repetitions of `يَوْمَئِذٍ` in 99:4 and 99:6 provide an explicit temporal frame. At 99:6, `ن و س/B001` contributes swaying or wavering to `ٱلنَّاسُ`, and `ش ت ت/B001` contributes collective scattering. These branches assign the fixed quake a propagating role: lower ground oscillates, the human collective becomes unstable, and the group separates. The causal propagation from substrate to crowd is inferred, while the roots and sequence are packet-supplied. The quake consequently reads as a shock of time reorganizing society as well as a movement of earth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B008","root_000059/B001","root_000638/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7804835f4bd03a033a01","text":"2. **The ground trembles like the human who will tremble.** Lexical evidence: `ء ر ض B008` trembling/shivering in a person; `ز ل ز ل B001` shaking; activated by `ء ن س B001/B002` visible human presence/perception in 99:3 and `ن و س B001` swaying instability in 99:6. Structural cue: the earth’s agitation precedes human bewildered speech and people’s issuing in unstable groups. Assigned role: the earth’s shaking becomes a bodily analogue for human destabilization. Abductive move: I supply a cross-domain mapping from ground-shake to person-shiver because the same packet makes earth and human both actors. Reading change: “the earth is shaken” also reads as the world assuming the body-language of humans who will face its evidence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000638/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_86fa7c22278ab40db6cd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:1:2\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:1:3\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:1:4\",\"root_000638\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sarsıntı, yeri edilgin maddeden etkin tanığa dönüştüren kanıtlama sürecinin ilk işlemi olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_96836858c25f0e18e6fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:1:2\",\"root_000638\",[\"B002\"]],[\"99:1:4\",\"root_000638\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Fiziksel deprem, gün vurgusuyla zamanın birikmiş şoklarını belirleyici bir krizde yoğunlaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dbc0432c4426f0eebe2d","text":"1. **The ground is rendered as a trembling body.** Fixed `ز ل ز ل/B001` supplies shaking disturbance, while fixed `ء ر ض/B008` unexpectedly supplies trembling or shivering in a person. Structurally, the repeated root in `زُلْزِلَتِ ... زِلْزَالَهَا` encloses `ٱلْأَرْضُ` and gives the disturbance as its own suffixed quake. The earth branch's role is somatic: it makes the subject experience the motion as a body would. The projection of a human tremor onto the earth is the abductive move. The ayah then reads as the earth bodily trembling its particular tremor, not merely as terrain receiving external motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000638/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f50d5c864c2bb56be1fa","text":"2. **The passive shock is a threshold into active testimony.** Fixed `ز ل ز ل/B001` supplies the initial disturbance and fixed `ء ر ض/B001` the lower substrate. The fixed ayah presents the earth as the patient of a passive verb; 99:2 and 99:4 then give it active feminine verbs, `أَخْرَجَتِ` and `تُحَدِّثُ`, and 99:5 supplies the commission `أَوْحَىٰ لَهَا`. The quake's assigned role is to open the transition from affected matter to acting witness. The packet does not state that shaking enables speech, so that directional arrow is inferred.
